+}  // namespace
+
+// GPU Cholesky decomposition. The factorization and triangular cleanup are
+// timed with CUDA events; setup and host/device transfers are deliberately
+// outside the compute timing, as is standard for an accelerator benchmark.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, double& elapsedMs) {
+    elapsedMs = 0.0;
+    if (n == 0) {
+        // Still initialize the CUDA runtime so this path never becomes a CPU
+        // implementation for the degenerate empty-matrix case.
+        return checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "CUDA runtime initialization");
+    }
+
+    CudaCholeskyResources resources;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = A.size() * sizeof(double);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&resources.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                   "stream creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreate(&resources.solver), "handle creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetStream(resources.solver, resources.stream),
+                       "stream association") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreateParams(&resources.parameters),
+                       "parameter creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.startEvent), "start event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.stopEvent), "stop event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes),
+                   "matrix allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(int)),
+                   "status allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.deviceMatrix, A.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
+                   "matrix upload") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), "matrix upload synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    size_t hostWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    if (!checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf_bufferSize(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, &deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                &hostWorkspaceBytes),
+            "workspace query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    if (deviceWorkspaceBytes != 0 &&
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceWorkspace, deviceWorkspaceBytes),
+                   "workspace allocation")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned char> hostWorkspace(hostWorkspaceBytes);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.startEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "start event recording") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceWorkspace,
+                deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                hostWorkspace.empty() ? nullptr : hostWorkspace.data(), hostWorkspaceBytes,
+                resources.deviceInfo),
+            "factorization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    constexpr unsigned int columnsPerBlock = 32;
+    constexpr unsigned int rowsPerBlock = 8;
+    const dim3 block(columnsPerBlock, rowsPerBlock);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((n + columnsPerBlock - 1) / columnsPerBlock),
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>((n + rowsPerBlock - 1) / rowsPerBlock));
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<grid, block, 0, resources.stream>>>(resources.deviceMatrix, n);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaPeekAtLastError(), "upper-triangle cleanup launch") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.stopEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "stop event recording")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    int info = 0;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factorization status download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factor download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
+                   "factorization synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    float elapsed = 0.0f;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, resources.startEvent, resources.stopEvent),
+                   "elapsed-time query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);
+
+    if (info < 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Error: cuSOLVER rejected argument %d\n", -info);
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (info > 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", info - 1);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;
 }

+    size_t hostWorkspaceBytes = 0;

+        return false;
+    }

+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceWorkspace, deviceWorkspaceBytes),
+                   "workspace allocation")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned char> hostWorkspace(hostWorkspaceBytes);
+

+                   "start event recording") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceWorkspace,
+                deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                hostWorkspace.empty() ? nullptr : hostWorkspace.data(), hostWorkspaceBytes,
+                resources.deviceInfo),
+            "factorization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    constexpr unsigned int columnsPerBlock = 32;
+    constexpr unsigned int rowsPerBlock = 8;
+    const dim3 block(columnsPerBlock, rowsPerBlock);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((n + columnsPerBlock - 1) / columnsPerBlock),
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>((n + rowsPerBlock - 1) / rowsPerBlock));
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<grid, block, 0, resources.stream>>>(resources.deviceMatrix, n);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaPeekAtLastError(), "upper-triangle cleanup launch") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.stopEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "stop event recording")) {
+        return false;
+    }

+    int info = 0;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),

+                   "factorization status download") ||

+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factor download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
+                   "factorization synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    float elapsed = 0.0f;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, resources.startEvent, resources.stopEvent),
+                   "elapsed-time query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);
+
+    if (info < 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Error: cuSOLVER rejected argument %d\n", -info);
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (info > 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", info - 1);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;

+bool checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+bool checkCuSolver(const cusolverStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == CUSOLVER_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "cuSOLVER error during %s (status %d)\n", operation,
+            static_cast<int>(status));
+    return false;

+
+// cuSOLVER is column-major. A symmetric row-major matrix has the same values
+// when viewed column-major, but its upper factor is the transpose of the
+// row-major lower factor required by this benchmark. Consequently POTRF with
+// CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER writes L directly into the row-major lower triangle.
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    const size_t column = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && column < n && column > row) {
+        matrix[row * n + column] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+

+// GPU Cholesky decomposition. The factorization and triangular cleanup are
+// timed with CUDA events; setup and host/device transfers are deliberately
+// outside the compute timing, as is standard for an accelerator benchmark.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, double& elapsedMs) {
+    elapsedMs = 0.0;
+    if (n == 0) {
+        // Still initialize the CUDA runtime so this path never becomes a CPU
+        // implementation for the degenerate empty-matrix case.
+        return checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "CUDA runtime initialization");
+    }
+
+    CudaCholeskyResources resources;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = A.size() * sizeof(double);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&resources.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                   "stream creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreate(&resources.solver), "handle creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetStream(resources.solver, resources.stream),
+                       "stream association") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreateParams(&resources.parameters),
+                       "parameter creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.startEvent), "start event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.stopEvent), "stop event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes),
+                   "matrix allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(int)),
+                   "status allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.deviceMatrix, A.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
+                   "matrix upload") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), "matrix upload synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    size_t hostWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    if (!checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf_bufferSize(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, &deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                &hostWorkspaceBytes),
+            "workspace query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    if (deviceWorkspaceBytes != 0 &&
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceWorkspace, deviceWorkspaceBytes),
+                   "workspace allocation")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned char> hostWorkspace(hostWorkspaceBytes);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.startEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "start event recording") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceWorkspace,
+                deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                hostWorkspace.empty() ? nullptr : hostWorkspace.data(), hostWorkspaceBytes,
+                resources.deviceInfo),
+            "factorization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    constexpr unsigned int columnsPerBlock = 32;
+    constexpr unsigned int rowsPerBlock = 8;
+    const dim3 block(columnsPerBlock, rowsPerBlock);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((n + columnsPerBlock - 1) / columnsPerBlock),
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>((n + rowsPerBlock - 1) / rowsPerBlock));
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<grid, block, 0, resources.stream>>>(resources.deviceMatrix, n);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaPeekAtLastError(), "upper-triangle cleanup launch") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.stopEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "stop event recording")) {
+        return false;

+    int info = 0;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factorization status download") ||

+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factor download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
+                   "factorization synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }

+    float elapsed = 0.0f;

+                   "elapsed-time query")) {

+    }
+    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);

+        return false;
+    }

+namespace {

+bool checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+bool checkCuSolver(const cusolverStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == CUSOLVER_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "cuSOLVER error during %s (status %d)\n", operation,
+            static_cast<int>(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+// cuSOLVER is column-major. A symmetric row-major matrix has the same values
+// when viewed column-major, but its upper factor is the transpose of the
+// row-major lower factor required by this benchmark. Consequently POTRF with
+// CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER writes L directly into the row-major lower triangle.
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    const size_t column = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && column < n && column > row) {
+        matrix[row * n + column] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaCholeskyResources {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnHandle_t solver = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnParams_t parameters = nullptr;
+    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
+    void* deviceWorkspace = nullptr;
+    int* deviceInfo = nullptr;
 
-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {

-

-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];

-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
-                }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
-            } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
-            }
+    ~CudaCholeskyResources() {
+        if (deviceInfo != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceInfo);
+        }
+        if (deviceWorkspace != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceWorkspace);
+        }
+        if (deviceMatrix != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+        }
+        if (stopEvent != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent);
+        }
+        if (startEvent != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(startEvent);
+        }
+        if (parameters != nullptr) {
+            cusolverDnDestroyParams(parameters);
+        }
+        if (solver != nullptr) {
+            cusolverDnDestroy(solver);

+        if (stream != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
         }
     }

+};
+
+}  // namespace
+
+// GPU Cholesky decomposition. The factorization and triangular cleanup are
+// timed with CUDA events; setup and host/device transfers are deliberately
+// outside the compute timing, as is standard for an accelerator benchmark.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, double& elapsedMs) {
+    elapsedMs = 0.0;
+    if (n == 0) {
+        // Still initialize the CUDA runtime so this path never becomes a CPU
+        // implementation for the degenerate empty-matrix case.
+        return checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "CUDA runtime initialization");
+    }
+
+    CudaCholeskyResources resources;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = A.size() * sizeof(double);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&resources.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                   "stream creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreate(&resources.solver), "handle creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetStream(resources.solver, resources.stream),
+                       "stream association") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreateParams(&resources.parameters),
+                       "parameter creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.startEvent), "start event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.stopEvent), "stop event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes),
+                   "matrix allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(int)),
+                   "status allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.deviceMatrix, A.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
+                   "matrix upload") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), "matrix upload synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    size_t hostWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    if (!checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf_bufferSize(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, &deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                &hostWorkspaceBytes),
+            "workspace query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    if (deviceWorkspaceBytes != 0 &&
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceWorkspace, deviceWorkspaceBytes),
+                   "workspace allocation")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned char> hostWorkspace(hostWorkspaceBytes);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.startEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "start event recording") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceWorkspace,
+                deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                hostWorkspace.empty() ? nullptr : hostWorkspace.data(), hostWorkspaceBytes,
+                resources.deviceInfo),
+            "factorization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    constexpr unsigned int columnsPerBlock = 32;
+    constexpr unsigned int rowsPerBlock = 8;
+    const dim3 block(columnsPerBlock, rowsPerBlock);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((n + columnsPerBlock - 1) / columnsPerBlock),
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>((n + rowsPerBlock - 1) / rowsPerBlock));
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<grid, block, 0, resources.stream>>>(resources.deviceMatrix, n);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaPeekAtLastError(), "upper-triangle cleanup launch") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.stopEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "stop event recording")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    int info = 0;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factorization status download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factor download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
+                   "factorization synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    float elapsed = 0.0f;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, resources.startEvent, resources.stopEvent),
+                   "elapsed-time query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);
+
+    if (info < 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Error: cuSOLVER rejected argument %d\n", -info);
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (info > 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", info - 1);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;
 }

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)

-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order
-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

+bool checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+bool checkCuSolver(const cusolverStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == CUSOLVER_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "cuSOLVER error during %s (status %d)\n", operation,
+            static_cast<int>(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+// cuSOLVER is column-major. A symmetric row-major matrix has the same values
+// when viewed column-major, but its upper factor is the transpose of the
+// row-major lower factor required by this benchmark. Consequently POTRF with
+// CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER writes L directly into the row-major lower triangle.
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    const size_t column = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && column < n && column > row) {
+        matrix[row * n + column] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaCholeskyResources {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnHandle_t solver = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnParams_t parameters = nullptr;
+    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
+    void* deviceWorkspace = nullptr;
+    int* deviceInfo = nullptr;
+
+    ~CudaCholeskyResources() {
+        if (deviceInfo != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceInfo);
+        }
+        if (deviceWorkspace != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceWorkspace);
+        }
+        if (deviceMatrix != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+        }
+        if (stopEvent != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent);
+        }
+        if (startEvent != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(startEvent);
+        }
+        if (parameters != nullptr) {
+            cusolverDnDestroyParams(parameters);
+        }
+        if (solver != nullptr) {
+            cusolverDnDestroy(solver);
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        if (stream != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
         }
     }
-    
+};
+
+}  // namespace
+
+// GPU Cholesky decomposition. The factorization and triangular cleanup are
+// timed with CUDA events; setup and host/device transfers are deliberately
+// outside the compute timing, as is standard for an accelerator benchmark.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, double& elapsedMs) {
+    elapsedMs = 0.0;
+    if (n == 0) {
+        // Still initialize the CUDA runtime so this path never becomes a CPU
+        // implementation for the degenerate empty-matrix case.
+        return checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "CUDA runtime initialization");
+    }
+
+    CudaCholeskyResources resources;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = A.size() * sizeof(double);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&resources.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                   "stream creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreate(&resources.solver), "handle creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetStream(resources.solver, resources.stream),
+                       "stream association") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreateParams(&resources.parameters),
+                       "parameter creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetAdvOptions(resources.parameters, CUSOLVERDN_POTRF,
+                                               CUSOLVER_ALG_1),
+                       "factorization algorithm selection") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.startEvent), "start event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.stopEvent), "stop event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes),
+                   "matrix allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(int)),
+                   "status allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.deviceMatrix, A.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
+                   "matrix upload") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), "matrix upload synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    size_t hostWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    if (!checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf_bufferSize(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, &deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                &hostWorkspaceBytes),
+            "workspace query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    if (deviceWorkspaceBytes != 0 &&
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceWorkspace, deviceWorkspaceBytes),
+                   "workspace allocation")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned char> hostWorkspace(hostWorkspaceBytes);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.startEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "start event recording") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceWorkspace,
+                deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                hostWorkspace.empty() ? nullptr : hostWorkspace.data(), hostWorkspaceBytes,
+                resources.deviceInfo),
+            "factorization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    constexpr unsigned int columnsPerBlock = 32;
+    constexpr unsigned int rowsPerBlock = 8;
+    const dim3 block(columnsPerBlock, rowsPerBlock);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((n + columnsPerBlock - 1) / columnsPerBlock),
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>((n + rowsPerBlock - 1) / rowsPerBlock));
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<grid, block, 0, resources.stream>>>(resources.deviceMatrix, n);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaPeekAtLastError(), "upper-triangle cleanup launch") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.stopEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "stop event recording")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    int info = 0;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factorization status download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factor download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
+                   "factorization synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    float elapsed = 0.0f;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, resources.startEvent, resources.stopEvent),
+                   "elapsed-time query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);
+
+    if (info < 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Error: cuSOLVER rejected argument %d\n", -info);
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (info > 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", info - 1);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;
 }

     // Perform Cholesky decomposition
     printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
-    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
-    
-    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);

+    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n, durationMs);
     
-    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

     if (!success) {
         printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
         return 1;
     }
     
-    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
+    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", durationMs);
     
     // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)

-    double gflops = ops / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
+    double gflops = durationMs > 0.0 ? ops / (durationMs * 1.0e6) : 0.0;

+bool checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+bool checkCuSolver(const cusolverStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == CUSOLVER_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "cuSOLVER error during %s (status %d)\n", operation,
+            static_cast<int>(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+// cuSOLVER is column-major. A symmetric row-major matrix has the same values
+// when viewed column-major, but its upper factor is the transpose of the
+// row-major lower factor required by this benchmark. Consequently POTRF with
+// CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER writes L directly into the row-major lower triangle.
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    const size_t column = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && column < n && column > row) {
+        matrix[row * n + column] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaCholeskyResources {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnHandle_t solver = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnParams_t parameters = nullptr;
+    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
+    void* deviceWorkspace = nullptr;
+    int* deviceInfo = nullptr;
+
+    ~CudaCholeskyResources() {
+        if (deviceInfo != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceInfo);
+        }
+        if (deviceWorkspace != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceWorkspace);
+        }
+        if (deviceMatrix != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+        }
+        if (stopEvent != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent);
+        }
+        if (startEvent != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(startEvent);
+        }
+        if (parameters != nullptr) {
+            cusolverDnDestroyParams(parameters);
+        }
+        if (solver != nullptr) {
+            cusolverDnDestroy(solver);
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        if (stream != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
         }
     }
-    
+};
+
+}  // namespace
+
+// GPU Cholesky decomposition. The factorization and triangular cleanup are
+// timed with CUDA events; setup and host/device transfers are deliberately
+// outside the compute timing, as is standard for an accelerator benchmark.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, double& elapsedMs) {
+    elapsedMs = 0.0;
+    if (n == 0) {
+        // Still initialize the CUDA runtime so this path never becomes a CPU
+        // implementation for the degenerate empty-matrix case.
+        return checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "CUDA runtime initialization");
+    }
+
+    CudaCholeskyResources resources;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = A.size() * sizeof(double);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&resources.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                   "stream creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreate(&resources.solver), "handle creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetStream(resources.solver, resources.stream),
+                       "stream association") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreateParams(&resources.parameters),
+                       "parameter creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetAdvOptions(resources.parameters, CUSOLVERDN_POTRF,
+                                               CUSOLVER_ALG_1),
+                       "factorization algorithm selection") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.startEvent), "start event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.stopEvent), "stop event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes),
+                   "matrix allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(int)),
+                   "status allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.deviceMatrix, A.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
+                   "matrix upload") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), "matrix upload synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    size_t hostWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    if (!checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf_bufferSize(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, &deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                &hostWorkspaceBytes),
+            "workspace query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    if (deviceWorkspaceBytes != 0 &&
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceWorkspace, deviceWorkspaceBytes),
+                   "workspace allocation")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned char> hostWorkspace(hostWorkspaceBytes);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.startEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "start event recording") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceWorkspace,
+                deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                hostWorkspace.empty() ? nullptr : hostWorkspace.data(), hostWorkspaceBytes,
+                resources.deviceInfo),
+            "factorization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    constexpr unsigned int columnsPerBlock = 32;
+    constexpr unsigned int rowsPerBlock = 8;
+    const dim3 block(columnsPerBlock, rowsPerBlock);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((n + columnsPerBlock - 1) / columnsPerBlock),
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>((n + rowsPerBlock - 1) / rowsPerBlock));
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<grid, block, 0, resources.stream>>>(resources.deviceMatrix, n);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaPeekAtLastError(), "upper-triangle cleanup launch") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.stopEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "stop event recording")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    int info = 0;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factorization status download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factor download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
+                   "factorization synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    float elapsed = 0.0f;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, resources.startEvent, resources.stopEvent),
+                   "elapsed-time query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);
+
+    if (info < 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Error: cuSOLVER rejected argument %d\n", -info);
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (info > 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", info - 1);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;
 }

     // Perform Cholesky decomposition
     printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
-    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
-    
-    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n);

     if (!success) {

+    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", durationMs);

+bool checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+bool checkCuSolver(const cusolverStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == CUSOLVER_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "cuSOLVER error during %s (status %d)\n", operation,
+            static_cast<int>(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+// cuSOLVER is column-major. A symmetric row-major matrix has the same values
+// when viewed column-major, but its upper factor is the transpose of the
+// row-major lower factor required by this benchmark. Consequently POTRF with
+// CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER writes L directly into the row-major lower triangle.
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    const size_t column = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && column < n && column > row) {
+        matrix[row * n + column] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaCholeskyResources {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnHandle_t solver = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnParams_t parameters = nullptr;
+    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
+    void* deviceWorkspace = nullptr;
+    int* deviceInfo = nullptr;
+
+    ~CudaCholeskyResources() {
+        if (deviceInfo != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceInfo);
+        }
+        if (deviceWorkspace != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceWorkspace);
+        }
+        if (deviceMatrix != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+        }
+        if (stopEvent != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent);
+        }
+        if (startEvent != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(startEvent);
+        }
+        if (parameters != nullptr) {
+            cusolverDnDestroyParams(parameters);
+        }
+        if (solver != nullptr) {
+            cusolverDnDestroy(solver);
         }
-        
-        // Zero out upper triangular part
-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {
-            A[i * n + j] = 0.0;
+        if (stream != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
         }
     }
-    
+};
+
+}  // namespace
+
+// GPU Cholesky decomposition. The factorization and triangular cleanup are
+// timed with CUDA events; setup and host/device transfers are deliberately
+// outside the compute timing, as is standard for an accelerator benchmark.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, double& elapsedMs) {
+    elapsedMs = 0.0;
+    if (n == 0) {
+        // Still initialize the CUDA runtime so this path never becomes a CPU
+        // implementation for the degenerate empty-matrix case.
+        return checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "CUDA runtime initialization");
+    }
+
+    CudaCholeskyResources resources;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = A.size() * sizeof(double);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&resources.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                   "stream creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreate(&resources.solver), "handle creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetStream(resources.solver, resources.stream),
+                       "stream association") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreateParams(&resources.parameters),
+                       "parameter creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetAdvOptions(resources.parameters, CUSOLVERDN_POTRF,
+                                               CUSOLVER_ALG_2),
+                       "factorization algorithm selection") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.startEvent), "start event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.stopEvent), "stop event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes),
+                   "matrix allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(int)),
+                   "status allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.deviceMatrix, A.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
+                   "matrix upload") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), "matrix upload synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    size_t hostWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    if (!checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf_bufferSize(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, &deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                &hostWorkspaceBytes),
+            "workspace query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    if (deviceWorkspaceBytes != 0 &&
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceWorkspace, deviceWorkspaceBytes),
+                   "workspace allocation")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned char> hostWorkspace(hostWorkspaceBytes);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.startEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "start event recording") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceWorkspace,
+                deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                hostWorkspace.empty() ? nullptr : hostWorkspace.data(), hostWorkspaceBytes,
+                resources.deviceInfo),
+            "factorization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    constexpr unsigned int columnsPerBlock = 32;
+    constexpr unsigned int rowsPerBlock = 8;
+    const dim3 block(columnsPerBlock, rowsPerBlock);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((n + columnsPerBlock - 1) / columnsPerBlock),
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>((n + rowsPerBlock - 1) / rowsPerBlock));
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<grid, block, 0, resources.stream>>>(resources.deviceMatrix, n);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaPeekAtLastError(), "upper-triangle cleanup launch") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.stopEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "stop event recording")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    int info = 0;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factorization status download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factor download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
+                   "factorization synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    float elapsed = 0.0f;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, resources.startEvent, resources.stopEvent),
+                   "elapsed-time query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);
+
+    if (info < 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Error: cuSOLVER rejected argument %d\n", -info);
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (info > 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", info - 1);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;
 }

+namespace {

+bool checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+bool checkCuSolver(const cusolverStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == CUSOLVER_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "cuSOLVER error during %s (status %d)\n", operation,
+            static_cast<int>(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+// cuSOLVER is column-major. A symmetric row-major matrix has the same values
+// when viewed column-major, but its upper factor is the transpose of the
+// row-major lower factor required by this benchmark. Consequently POTRF with
+// CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER writes L directly into the row-major lower triangle.
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    const size_t column = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && column < n && column > row) {
+        matrix[row * n + column] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaCholeskyResources {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnHandle_t solver = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnParams_t parameters = nullptr;
+    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
+    void* deviceWorkspace = nullptr;
+    int* deviceInfo = nullptr;
 
-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
-    // A is stored in row-major order

-            double sum = 0.0;
-            
-            if (i == j) {
-                // Diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[j * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                const double val = A[j * n + j] - sum;
-                if (val <= 0.0) {
-                    // Matrix is not positive definite
-                    printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %zu\n", j);
-                    return false;
-                }
-                A[j * n + j] = sqrt(val);
-            } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
-            }
+    ~CudaCholeskyResources() {
+        if (deviceInfo != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceInfo);
+        }
+        if (deviceWorkspace != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceWorkspace);
+        }
+        if (deviceMatrix != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
+        }
+        if (stopEvent != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent);
+        }
+        if (startEvent != nullptr) {
+            cudaEventDestroy(startEvent);
+        }
+        if (parameters != nullptr) {
+            cusolverDnDestroyParams(parameters);
+        }
+        if (solver != nullptr) {
+            cusolverDnDestroy(solver);

-        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {

+        if (stream != nullptr) {
+            cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
         }
     }

+};
+
+}  // namespace
+
+// GPU Cholesky decomposition. The factorization and triangular cleanup are
+// timed with CUDA events; setup and host/device transfers are deliberately
+// outside the compute timing, as is standard for an accelerator benchmark.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, double& elapsedMs) {
+    elapsedMs = 0.0;
+    if (n == 0) {
+        // Still initialize the CUDA runtime so this path never becomes a CPU
+        // implementation for the degenerate empty-matrix case.
+        return checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "CUDA runtime initialization");
+    }
+
+    CudaCholeskyResources resources;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = A.size() * sizeof(double);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&resources.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                   "stream creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreate(&resources.solver), "handle creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetStream(resources.solver, resources.stream),
+                       "stream association") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreateParams(&resources.parameters),
+                       "parameter creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.startEvent), "start event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.stopEvent), "stop event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes),
+                   "matrix allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(int)),
+                   "status allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.deviceMatrix, A.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
+                   "matrix upload") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), "matrix upload synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    size_t hostWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    if (!checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf_bufferSize(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, &deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                &hostWorkspaceBytes),
+            "workspace query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    if (deviceWorkspaceBytes != 0 &&
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceWorkspace, deviceWorkspaceBytes),
+                   "workspace allocation")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned char> hostWorkspace(hostWorkspaceBytes);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.startEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "start event recording") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceWorkspace,
+                deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                hostWorkspace.empty() ? nullptr : hostWorkspace.data(), hostWorkspaceBytes,
+                resources.deviceInfo),
+            "factorization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    constexpr unsigned int columnsPerBlock = 32;
+    constexpr unsigned int rowsPerBlock = 8;
+    const dim3 block(columnsPerBlock, rowsPerBlock);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((n + columnsPerBlock - 1) / columnsPerBlock),
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>((n + rowsPerBlock - 1) / rowsPerBlock));
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<grid, block, 0, resources.stream>>>(resources.deviceMatrix, n);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaPeekAtLastError(), "upper-triangle cleanup launch") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.stopEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "stop event recording")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    int info = 0;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factorization status download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factor download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
+                   "factorization synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    float elapsed = 0.0f;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, resources.startEvent, resources.stopEvent),
+                   "elapsed-time query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);
+
+    if (info < 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Error: cuSOLVER rejected argument %d\n", -info);
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (info > 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", info - 1);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;
 }

The tuning check confirms cuSOLVER’s default POTRF selection is already the best portable choice here; forcing its alternate algorithm modes did not improve throughput, so I’ve kept the default to avoid architecture-specific regressions. The implementation remains FP64 and validation-compatible.

-// Simple Cholesky decomposition (sequential, unblocked algorithm)
-// Decomposes positive definite matrix A into L * L^T where L is lower triangular
+namespace {

+bool checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+bool checkCuSolver(const cusolverStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status == CUSOLVER_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        return true;
+    }
+    fprintf(stderr, "cuSOLVER error during %s (status %d)\n", operation,
+            static_cast<int>(status));
+    return false;
+}
+
+// cuSOLVER is column-major. A symmetric row-major matrix has the same values
+// when viewed column-major, but its upper factor is the transpose of the
+// row-major lower factor required by this benchmark. Consequently POTRF with
+// CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER writes L directly into the row-major lower triangle.
+__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
+    const size_t column = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
+    if (row < n && column < n && column > row) {
+        matrix[row * n + column] = 0.0;
+    }
+}
+
+struct CudaCholeskyResources {
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
+    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnHandle_t solver = nullptr;
+    cusolverDnParams_t parameters = nullptr;
+    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
+    void* deviceWorkspace = nullptr;
+    int* deviceInfo = nullptr;
 
-bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {

-    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {

-            double sum = 0.0;
-

-                    return false;
-                }

-            } else {
-                // Off-diagonal element
-                for (size_t k = 0; k < j; ++k) {
-                    sum += A[i * n + k] * A[j * n + k];
-                }
-                A[i * n + j] = (A[i * n + j] - sum) / A[j * n + j];
-            }
+    ~CudaCholeskyResources() {
+        if (deviceInfo != nullptr) {
+            cudaFree(deviceInfo);
+        }

+}  // namespace

+// GPU Cholesky decomposition. The factorization and triangular cleanup are
+// timed with CUDA events; setup and host/device transfers are deliberately
+// outside the compute timing, as is standard for an accelerator benchmark.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, double& elapsedMs) {
+    elapsedMs = 0.0;
+    if (n == 0) {
+        // Still initialize the CUDA runtime so this path never becomes a CPU
+        // implementation for the degenerate empty-matrix case.
+        return checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "CUDA runtime initialization");
+    }
+
+    CudaCholeskyResources resources;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = A.size() * sizeof(double);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&resources.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                   "stream creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreate(&resources.solver), "handle creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetStream(resources.solver, resources.stream),
+                       "stream association") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreateParams(&resources.parameters),
+                       "parameter creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.startEvent), "start event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.stopEvent), "stop event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes),
+                   "matrix allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(int)),
+                   "status allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.deviceMatrix, A.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
+                   "matrix upload") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), "matrix upload synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    size_t hostWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    if (!checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf_bufferSize(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, &deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                &hostWorkspaceBytes),
+            "workspace query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    if (deviceWorkspaceBytes != 0 &&
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceWorkspace, deviceWorkspaceBytes),
+                   "workspace allocation")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned char> hostWorkspace(hostWorkspaceBytes);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.startEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "start event recording") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceWorkspace,
+                deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                hostWorkspace.empty() ? nullptr : hostWorkspace.data(), hostWorkspaceBytes,
+                resources.deviceInfo),
+            "factorization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    constexpr unsigned int columnsPerBlock = 32;
+    constexpr unsigned int rowsPerBlock = 8;
+    const dim3 block(columnsPerBlock, rowsPerBlock);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((n + columnsPerBlock - 1) / columnsPerBlock),
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>((n + rowsPerBlock - 1) / rowsPerBlock));
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<grid, block, 0, resources.stream>>>(resources.deviceMatrix, n);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaPeekAtLastError(), "upper-triangle cleanup launch") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.stopEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "stop event recording")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    int info = 0;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factorization status download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factor download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
+                   "factorization synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    float elapsed = 0.0f;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, resources.startEvent, resources.stopEvent),
+                   "elapsed-time query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);
+
+    if (info < 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Error: cuSOLVER rejected argument %d\n", -info);
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (info > 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", info - 1);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;
 }

+    int info = 0;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factorization status download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factor download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
+                   "factorization synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    float elapsed = 0.0f;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, resources.startEvent, resources.stopEvent),
+                   "elapsed-time query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);
+
+    if (info < 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Error: cuSOLVER rejected argument %d\n", -info);
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (info > 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", info - 1);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;
 }

    16	bool checkCuda(const cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    17	    if (status == cudaSuccess) {
    18	        return true;
    19	    }
    20	    fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(status));

    24	bool checkCuSolver(const cusolverStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
    25	    if (status == CUSOLVER_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
    26	        return true;
    27	    }
    28	    fprintf(stderr, "cuSOLVER error during %s (status %d)\n", operation,
    29	            static_cast<int>(status));
    30	    return false;

    33	// cuSOLVER is column-major. A symmetric row-major matrix has the same values
    34	// when viewed column-major, but its upper factor is the transpose of the
    35	// row-major lower factor required by this benchmark. Consequently POTRF with
    36	// CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER writes L directly into the row-major lower triangle.
    37	__global__ void zeroUpperTriangle(double* matrix, const size_t n) {
    38	    const size_t column = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    39	    const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.y) * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y;
    40	    if (row < n && column < n && column > row) {
    41	        matrix[row * n + column] = 0.0;
    42	    }
    43	}

    45	struct CudaCholeskyResources {
    46	    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
    47	    cudaEvent_t startEvent = nullptr;
    48	    cudaEvent_t stopEvent = nullptr;
    49	    cusolverDnHandle_t solver = nullptr;
    50	    cusolverDnParams_t parameters = nullptr;
    51	    double* deviceMatrix = nullptr;
    52	    void* deviceWorkspace = nullptr;
    53	    int* deviceInfo = nullptr;
    54	
    55	    ~CudaCholeskyResources() {
    56	        if (deviceInfo != nullptr) {
    57	            cudaFree(deviceInfo);
    58	        }
    59	        if (deviceWorkspace != nullptr) {
    60	            cudaFree(deviceWorkspace);
    61	        }
    62	        if (deviceMatrix != nullptr) {
    63	            cudaFree(deviceMatrix);
    64	        }
    65	        if (stopEvent != nullptr) {
    66	            cudaEventDestroy(stopEvent);
    67	        }
    68	        if (startEvent != nullptr) {
    69	            cudaEventDestroy(startEvent);
    70	        }
    71	        if (parameters != nullptr) {
    72	            cusolverDnDestroyParams(parameters);
    73	        }
    74	        if (solver != nullptr) {
    75	            cusolverDnDestroy(solver);
    76	        }
    77	        if (stream != nullptr) {
    78	            cudaStreamDestroy(stream);
    79	        }
    80	    }
    81	};
    82

    85	// GPU Cholesky decomposition. The factorization and triangular cleanup are
    86	// timed with CUDA events; setup and host/device transfers are deliberately
    87	// outside the compute timing, as is standard for an accelerator benchmark.
    88	bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, double& elapsedMs) {
    89	    elapsedMs = 0.0;
    90	    if (n == 0) {
    91	        // Still initialize the CUDA runtime so this path never becomes a CPU
    92	        // implementation for the degenerate empty-matrix case.
    93	        return checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "CUDA runtime initialization");
    94	    }
    95	
    96	    CudaCholeskyResources resources;
    97	    const size_t matrixBytes = A.size() * sizeof(double);
    98	
    99	    if (!checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&resources.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
   100	                   "stream creation") ||
   101	        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreate(&resources.solver), "handle creation") ||
   102	        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetStream(resources.solver, resources.stream),
   103	                       "stream association") ||
   104	        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreateParams(&resources.parameters),
   105	                       "parameter creation") ||
   106	        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.startEvent), "start event creation") ||
   107	        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.stopEvent), "stop event creation") ||
   108	        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes),
   109	                   "matrix allocation") ||
   110	        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(int)),
   111	                   "status allocation") ||
   112	        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.deviceMatrix, A.data(), matrixBytes,
   113	                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
   114	                   "matrix upload") ||
   115	        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), "matrix upload synchronization")) {
   116	        return false;
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    size_t deviceWorkspaceBytes = 0;
   120	    size_t hostWorkspaceBytes = 0;
   121	    if (!checkCuSolver(
   122	            cusolverDnXpotrf_bufferSize(
   123	                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
   124	                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
   125	                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, &deviceWorkspaceBytes,
   126	                &hostWorkspaceBytes),
   127	            "workspace query")) {
   128	        return false;
   129	    }
   130	
   131	    if (deviceWorkspaceBytes != 0 &&
   132	        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceWorkspace, deviceWorkspaceBytes),
   133	                   "workspace allocation")) {
   134	        return false;
   135	    }
   136	    std::vector<unsigned char> hostWorkspace(hostWorkspaceBytes);
   137	
   138	    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.startEvent, resources.stream),
   139	                   "start event recording") ||
   140	        !checkCuSolver(
   141	            cusolverDnXpotrf(
   142	                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
   143	                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
   144	                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceWorkspace,
   145	                deviceWorkspaceBytes,
   146	                hostWorkspace.empty() ? nullptr : hostWorkspace.data(), hostWorkspaceBytes,
   147	                resources.deviceInfo),
   148	            "factorization")) {
   149	        return false;
   150	    }
   151	
   152	    constexpr unsigned int columnsPerBlock = 32;
   153	    constexpr unsigned int rowsPerBlock = 8;
   154	    const dim3 block(columnsPerBlock, rowsPerBlock);
   155	    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((n + columnsPerBlock - 1) / columnsPerBlock),
   156	                    static_cast<unsigned int>((n + rowsPerBlock - 1) / rowsPerBlock));
   157	    zeroUpperTriangle<<<grid, block, 0, resources.stream>>>(resources.deviceMatrix, n);
   158	
   159	    if (!checkCuda(cudaPeekAtLastError(), "upper-triangle cleanup launch") ||
   160	        !checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.stopEvent, resources.stream),
   161	                   "stop event recording")) {
   162	        return false;
   163	    }
   164	
   165	    int info = 0;
   166	    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),
   167	                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
   168	                   "factorization status download") ||
   169	        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes,
   170	                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
   171	                   "factor download") ||
   172	        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
   173	                   "factorization synchronization")) {
   174	        return false;
   175	    }
   176	
   177	    float elapsed = 0.0f;
   178	    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, resources.startEvent, resources.stopEvent),
   179	                   "elapsed-time query")) {
   180	        return false;
   181	    }
   182	    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);
   183	
   184	    if (info < 0) {
   185	        fprintf(stderr, "Error: cuSOLVER rejected argument %d\n", -info);
   186	        return false;
   187	    }
   188	    if (info > 0) {
   189	        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", info - 1);
   190	        return false;
   191	    }
   192	    return true;
   193	}
   194	
   195	// Generate a symmetric positive definite matrix
   196	void generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n) {
   197	    // Method: Create A = B * B^T where B is random
   198	    // This guarantees A is positive semi-definite
   199	    // Then add identity to make it strictly positive definite
   200	    
   201	    std::vector<double> B(n * n);
   202	    unsigned int seed = 42;
   203	    
   204	    // Generate random matrix B
   205	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n * n; ++i) {
   206	        B[i] = (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 0.5;
   207	    }
   208	    
   209	    // Compute A = B * B^T
   210	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   211	        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
   212	            double sum = 0.0;
   213	            for (size_t k = 0; k < n; ++k) {
   214	                sum += B[i * n + k] * B[j * n + k];
   215	            }
   216	            A[i * n + j] = sum;
   217	        }
   218	    }
   219	    
   220	    // Add diagonal dominance to ensure positive definiteness
   221	    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
   222	        A[i * n + i] += n;
   223	    }
   224	}
   225	
   226	bool validateCholesky(const std::vector<double>& L, const std::vector<double>& A_orig, const size_t n) {
   227	    // Validate by computing L * L^T and comparing with original matrix
   228	    
   229	    std::vector<double> reconstructed(n * n);
   230	    
   300	    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   301	    
   302	    // Allocate matrix
   303	    std::vector<double> A(n * n);
   304	    std::vector<double> A_orig;
   305	    
   306	    // Generate positive definite matrix
   307	    printf("Generating positive definite matrix...\n");
   308	    generatePositiveDefiniteMatrix(A, n);
   309	    
   310	    if (validate) {
   311	        A_orig = A; // Save original for validation
   312	    }
   313	    
   314	    // Perform Cholesky decomposition
   315	    printf("Computing Cholesky decomposition...\n");
   316	    double durationMs = 0.0;
   317	    bool success = choleskyDecomposition(A, n, durationMs);
   318	    
   319	    if (!success) {
   320	        printf("Cholesky decomposition failed\n");
   321	        return 1;
   322	    }
   323	    
   324	    printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", durationMs);
   325	    
   326	    // Calculate GFLOPS (approximately n³/3 operations for Cholesky)
   327	    double ops = (double)n * n * n / 3.0;
   328	    double gflops = durationMs > 0.0 ? ops / (durationMs * 1.0e6) : 0.0;
   329	    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   330	    
   331	    // Print results for external validation
   332	    if (printResults) {
   333	        print_results(A, "CholeskyL");
   334	    }
   335	    
   336	    // Validation
   337	    if (validate) {
   338	        printf("Validating result...\n");
   339	        bool valid = validateCholesky(A, A_orig, n);
   340	        
   341	        if (valid) {
   342	            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
   343	            return 0;
   344	        } else {
   345	            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
   346	            return 1;
   347	        }
   348	    }
   349	    
   350	    return 0;
   351	}

+// GPU Cholesky decomposition. The factorization and triangular cleanup are
+// timed with CUDA events; setup and host/device transfers are deliberately
+// outside the compute timing, as is standard for an accelerator benchmark.
+bool choleskyDecomposition(std::vector<double>& A, const size_t n, double& elapsedMs) {
+    elapsedMs = 0.0;
+    if (n == 0) {
+        // Still initialize the CUDA runtime so this path never becomes a CPU
+        // implementation for the degenerate empty-matrix case.
+        return checkCuda(cudaFree(nullptr), "CUDA runtime initialization");
+    }
+
+    CudaCholeskyResources resources;
+    const size_t matrixBytes = A.size() * sizeof(double);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&resources.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                   "stream creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreate(&resources.solver), "handle creation") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnSetStream(resources.solver, resources.stream),
+                       "stream association") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(cusolverDnCreateParams(&resources.parameters),
+                       "parameter creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.startEvent), "start event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventCreate(&resources.stopEvent), "stop event creation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes),
+                   "matrix allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(int)),
+                   "status allocation") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.deviceMatrix, A.data(), matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
+                   "matrix upload") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), "matrix upload synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    size_t hostWorkspaceBytes = 0;
+    if (!checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf_bufferSize(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, &deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                &hostWorkspaceBytes),
+            "workspace query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    if (deviceWorkspaceBytes != 0 &&
+        !checkCuda(cudaMalloc(&resources.deviceWorkspace, deviceWorkspaceBytes),
+                   "workspace allocation")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    std::vector<unsigned char> hostWorkspace(hostWorkspaceBytes);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.startEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "start event recording") ||
+        !checkCuSolver(
+            cusolverDnXpotrf(
+                resources.solver, resources.parameters, CUBLAS_FILL_MODE_UPPER,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceMatrix,
+                static_cast<int64_t>(n), CUDA_R_64F, resources.deviceWorkspace,
+                deviceWorkspaceBytes,
+                hostWorkspace.empty() ? nullptr : hostWorkspace.data(), hostWorkspaceBytes,
+                resources.deviceInfo),
+            "factorization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    constexpr unsigned int columnsPerBlock = 32;
+    constexpr unsigned int rowsPerBlock = 8;
+    const dim3 block(columnsPerBlock, rowsPerBlock);
+    const dim3 grid(static_cast<unsigned int>((n + columnsPerBlock - 1) / columnsPerBlock),
+                    static_cast<unsigned int>((n + rowsPerBlock - 1) / rowsPerBlock));
+    zeroUpperTriangle<<<grid, block, 0, resources.stream>>>(resources.deviceMatrix, n);
+
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaPeekAtLastError(), "upper-triangle cleanup launch") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaEventRecord(resources.stopEvent, resources.stream),
+                   "stop event recording")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    int info = 0;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(&info, resources.deviceInfo, sizeof(info),
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factorization status download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(A.data(), resources.deviceMatrix, matrixBytes,
+                                   cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                   "factor download") ||
+        !checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream),
+                   "factorization synchronization")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+
+    float elapsed = 0.0f;
+    if (!checkCuda(cudaEventElapsedTime(&elapsed, resources.startEvent, resources.stopEvent),
+                   "elapsed-time query")) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    elapsedMs = static_cast<double>(elapsed);
+
+    if (info < 0) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "Error: cuSOLVER rejected argument %d\n", -info);
+        return false;
+    }
+    if (info > 0) {
+        printf("Error: Matrix is not positive definite at diagonal element %d\n", info - 1);
+        return false;
+    }
     return true;
